1,028 entries, react-security to README. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **react-security**: Frontend security reviewer persona for React apps. Use to review XSS risks, secret exposure in client bundles, token storage, auth/route guards, unsafe redirects, server/client data leaks (Next.js RSC, Server Actions), and dependency risks. ([riseshinee/superclaude-react](https://github.com/riseshinee/superclaude-react/blob/HEAD/agents/react-security.md))
- **react-security-agent**: Conducts OWASP-based security reviews of React / TypeScript front-end applications. Runs npm audit, eslint-plugin-security, and grep patterns across all OWASP Top 10 categories mapped to client-side risks (XSS via raw HTML, URL/protocol injection, bundle secrets, insecure token storage, dependency CVEs, missing CSP, open redirects). Generates manager-friendly executive summaries and technical findings tables. Use when asked to review React security, audit a frontend for vulnerabilities, check react XSS, OWASP react, npm audit review, or assess a React app's security posture. ([michaelalber/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/michaelalber/ai-toolkit/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/react-security-agent.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **react-security-auditing-architect**: Coworker agent specializing in React Security Auditing Architect ([UitbreidenOS/UitKit](https://github.com/UitbreidenOS/UitKit/blob/HEAD/plugins/uitkit-productivity/agents/react-security-auditing-architect.md) · ★14)
- **react-security-auditor**: Use to conduct a deep, codebase-wide security audit of a React app and produce a prioritized remediation report. Makes no edits (Bash is read-only by instruction, not sandboxed). Sweeps the entire codebase for threat-surface issues: XSS, dangerouslySetInnerHTML, client-exposed secrets, unvalidated redirects, and npm CVEs. Use proactively before releases, when adding auth or external I/O, or when handling sensitive data. ([makubexD/sigil](https://github.com/makubexD/sigil/blob/HEAD/catalog/languages/react/agents/react-security-auditor.agent.md))
- **react-security-wasp-drone**: Deep, evidence-backed security audit of React 18 and 19 code for /audit-my-code. Use when stack routing sends react (or nextjs, for client patterns), or when asked to check XSS sinks, SSR injection, RSC runtime CVEs such as React2Shell, Server Functions, bundle secrets, client-side trust, or AI-built React apps. Do NOT use for Next.js server surfaces, generic OWASP or git-history secret scans, other frameworks, or fixing code. - **reader**: Reads files, logs and command output on Haiku and returns a short answer with file:line quotes. Use when you need to know what is in a lot of text but not the text itself - "where is X defined", "what does this file/module do", "what failed in this log or test output", "list every place that does Y", a file sweep across many files, or a big saved output file. It has no shell: save a log or command output to a file first and give it the path. Diffs, PR reviews and summaries of them stay on Sonnet or Opus: not your job. Not for chat history (use history-reader), and not for reading a file you a… ([RishabhSFI/claude-harness-starter](https://github.com/RishabhSFI/claude-harness-starter/blob/HEAD/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Quarantined reader for tech-digest. Reads untrusted page text in build/reads/ and writes one action card per item to build/cards/&lt;id&gt;.json. - **reader**: Stage 1. Cold read of the full manuscript as a paying reader, not an editor. Produces reports/01-readers-report.md. Use first, before any editing; never argue with its output. ([saurabhmishra2290/book-publishing-pipeline](https://github.com/saurabhmishra2290/book-publishing-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Reads one selected work for the wiki from its archived text and writes what it establishes — a new note for a concept the wiki lacks, an addition to the note of a concept it already holds. Returns a short package. No web. Used by /grow. ([SertacCebeci/question-augment](https://github.com/SertacCebeci/question-augment/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Use PROACTIVELY when critically reading a specific paper, book, or chapter and recording structured notes into the Obsidian vault. Produces project-relevant critique plus atomic paper notes (library/papers/) and concept notes (library/concepts/). ([shusuke-ioku/r2](https://github.com/shusuke-ioku/r2/blob/HEAD/src/r2/template/.claude/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: You receive only the artifact path and reader questions as task data. Read and, when available, inspect the rendered artifact, starting with expanders closed. - **reader**: Reads one source at a stated depth and returns what it establishes, every point carrying a passage it has checked verbatim against the cached text. Use when a paper has to be understood rather than judged. Refuses to run on a source with no full text. ([tanyelai/nullius](https://github.com/tanyelai/nullius/blob/HEAD/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: 外部のリポジトリのソース、長いログやドキュメントを読み、問いへの答えを根拠つきで返す。確かめのためのスクラッチのスクリプトを書いて動かす作業や、採択された ADR とテストがそろった機械的な実装にも使う。 ([tett23/dotfiles](https://github.com/tett23/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Reads an App's code around the candidate usage locations of one package and returns a structured summary of how each location uses it and where its input comes from. - **reader**: Read-only worker for a meta-loop fan-out. Searches, reads and traces one bounded question, then returns findings as text - never files, never edits. - **reader-advocate**: The target-reader success gate — replaces the retired "Reader-Test" with a persona simulation, not a scoring dial. Use at PIPELINE step 8a READER-SIM, after Step 8 SCORE passes and before Step 9 FIGURES. Reads the chapter STRICTLY in-persona as the book's target reader with NO prior knowledge of the chapter's specific topic (per 00-strategy/AUDIENCE.md) and lists every spot that reader stalls, guesses, hits an unstated prerequisite, or cannot reproduce a result. Distinct from tech-clarity-reviewer, who reads as a subject expert. ([Pratiyush/book-java-code-quality](https://github.com/Pratiyush/book-java-code-quality/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader-advocate.md) · 2 copies)
- **reader-advocate**: Represents recruiters and hiring managers. Judges only the rendered site, PDFs and briefs, never source code. Use for external validation and phase-gate reader checks (no per-PR review since ADR-0026). ([Rohanjain2312/rohanjain.pro](https://github.com/Rohanjain2312/rohanjain.pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader-advocate.md))
- **reader-agent**: Simulate a first-time reader with no prior context to test whether a document is clear, complete, and unambiguous. - **reader-careful**: Comprehensive chapter analysis with craft observations and quotes. Includes all skim-level data plus arcs, themes, and evidence. ([howells/fiction](https://github.com/howells/fiction/blob/HEAD/agents/reader-careful.md) · ★24 · 2 copies)
- **reader-contract**: Every human-facing output from this repository must stand alone. The reader is an Azure engineer who has never seen this repository and is new to event-driven architecture, distributed systems, Kafka, Kafka Connect, and Debezium. The reader must be able to understand the result without an earlier chat turn or a required link. Governance review output posted to a pull request is exempt: its reader ([collaborationwithothers/cdc-platform-azure](https://github.com/collaborationwithothers/cdc-platform-azure/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/reader-contract.md))
- **reader-critic**: Use this agent to read finished output the way its reader will, aloud, and find what is false, overclaimed, misattributed, dead or repeated before it ships. It reads samples across a whole cycle and in several places, checks every claim against the sources and the computation, and returns numbered findings with fixes. Typical triggers: a feature's words and computations are integrated and sampled; a fix round answers earlier findings. It never edits files. ([Darv0n/WINENV](https://github.com/Darv0n/WINENV/blob/HEAD/configs/claude/agents/reader-critic.md))
- **reader-critic**: 読み手レビューの人。できあがった文章を、実際に読む人（応募者・受講生・店舗スタッフ・顧客）の目線で読み、わかりにくい・不安になる・読むのをやめたくなる箇所を指摘する。人が読む文章を作ったら公開前に必ず使う。 ([KRM-CORP/krm-claude-kit](https://github.com/KRM-CORP/krm-claude-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/krm-kit/agents/reader-critic.md))
- **reader-critic-checker**: 读者锐评检查器。以普通读者 + 编辑退稿双视角对章节 deep research，找问题给建议。 ([XuanRanL/webnovelwriter](https://github.com/XuanRanL/webnovelwriter/blob/HEAD/webnovel-writer/agents/reader-critic-checker.md) · ★31)
- **reader-deep**: Read-only deep analysis. Use for architecture or design audits, subtle bugs (concurrency, state, security), whole-branch review, or questions that need external docs alongside the code. ([twitchel/dotfiles](https://github.com/twitchel/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/home/dot_claude/agents/reader-deep.md) · ★2)
- **reader-deep** ([rezzminator/engine-sync](https://github.com/rezzminator/engine-sync/blob/HEAD/testdata/projects/integration/input/project/templates/global/agents/reader-deep.md) · ★1)
- **reader-digest**: Coordinate reading an entire manuscript via parallel reader agents. Writes manuscript-digest.md and returns a short summary. Prevents context overflow in parent agents. ([howells/fiction](https://github.com/howells/fiction/blob/HEAD/agents/reader-digest.md) · ★24 · 2 copies)
- **reader-digester**: Reads long files/plans/reports and returns a ≤300-word digest so a coordinator never spends its own context on a &gt;100-line read. Read-only; never edits. ([reubenjohn/reposix](https://github.com/reubenjohn/reposix/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader-digester.md) · ★1)
- **reader-editor**: 每次先读 [`references/team-orchestration.md`](../references/team-orchestration.md) §2、§4、§9（S8.5 的位置、限制分层与回退规则），再完整阅读 [`agents/book-writer.md`](book-writer.md) 的“文风”一节：那是本席唯一的改稿依据，本文件不另立文风规则。并读一遍 [`references/exemplars.md`](../references/exemplars.md)，它是改稿时“读者口吻”的参照。遵守 `../schemas/case_evidence.json` 与 `../schemas/chart_plan.json`。 ([Loveacup/jz-skills](https://github.com/Loveacup/jz-skills/blob/HEAD/shared/destiny-matrix/agents/reader-editor.md) · ★1)
- **reader-empathy-analyst**: 독자 관점에서 문서의 이해도 장벽을 분석하는 전문가. 초보자가 겪을 혼란, 개념 점프, 누락된 설명, 죽은 학습 경로를 식별한다. ([advanced-beginner/advanced-beginner.github.io](https://github.com/advanced-beginner/advanced-beginner.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader-empathy-analyst.md))
- **reader-engineer**: Implements changes in reader/ (the Python memory reader — IL2CPP resolution, offsets, metrics, run lifecycle, calibration/seed). The highest-risk area of the repo. Use for ANY reader change, however small. NOT for the Electron app (meter-engineer) and not for the game-update reseed playbook (meter-game-update skill). ([mad-labs-org/tbh-meter](https://github.com/mad-labs-org/tbh-meter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader-engineer.md) · ★39)
- **reader-eye**: Review a concall-alpha company-page section from a reader's perspective — does an analyst landing on the screen cold walk away with the takeaway the section was designed to deliver? Use after a section is redesigned, when a new section is added, or when the user explicitly asks "does this read right?" / "what does a reader get here?". Read-only — returns a structured reader-experience report grounded in the screenshot + JSX + schema + framework reader-intent doc, never edits files. ([pranav-9/concall-alpha](https://github.com/pranav-9/concall-alpha/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader-eye.md))
- **reader-feel**: 読書画面の手触り(3層の濃度、減速カーブ、地形の可読性、アニメーション)をスクリーンショットを見ながら調整する。M2以降で使う。 ([Crazykimwipe/Crest](https://github.com/Crazykimwipe/Crest/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader-feel.md))
- **reader-fidelity-reviewer**: Independently test a substantial or high-stakes draft for reader comprehension and fidelity to its source. Return findings only. ([tschuehly/skill-incubator](https://github.com/tschuehly/skill-incubator/blob/HEAD/skills/write-for-humans/agents/reader-fidelity-reviewer.md) · ★5)
- **reader-file**: Robowright Reader (reader) for one question about one or more files — given the file paths and the question, returns the lines that answer it, quoted with file:line, or "not found". Use when a file is too large to pull into the caller's context, as `/robowright help` does. Reads only; it runs nothing and writes nothing. Haiku, no rules loaded. ([AJschoons/robowright](https://github.com/AJschoons/robowright/blob/HEAD/agents/reader-file.md) · ★1)
- **reader-first-docs-reviewer**: Read-only audit of existing long-form docs (README, guides, CONTRIBUTING) against the reader-first-docs skill — computes TTV mechanically, flags install/abstraction-before-proof, redundancy, and runs the 30-second test. Returns a report plus a selective list of RESTRUCTURE-verdict findings; never files TODOs, never edits. ([cavo789/blog](https://github.com/cavo789/blog/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader-first-docs-reviewer.md) · ★17)
- **reader-forward**: plugin-lab fixture agent; reads the files it is given, one per step ([rezzminator/professor](https://github.com/rezzminator/professor/blob/HEAD/infra/plugin-lab/fixtures/lab-agents/plugins/lab-agents/agents/reader-forward.md) · ★9)
- **reader-graph**: Dispatch profile (Sonnet, medium). The reader profile plus four Graphify read tools (find, callers, impact, trace) - no shell, no file write, no tool search, no subagent. For the sa-triage triager. ([TOKYO-BEANS/claude-cookbook](https://github.com/TOKYO-BEANS/claude-cookbook/blob/HEAD/plugins/cookbook/agents/reader-graph.md))
- **reader-haiku**: Read-only lookup: find where something is defined or used and summarise it. Cheapest reader. ([mcmadsen/lean-agent](https://github.com/mcmadsen/lean-agent/blob/HEAD/plugins/lean-agent/agents/reader-haiku.md) · ★1)
- **reader-high**: pstack internal: read-only worker at high effort. ([irfanalimd/pstack-ports](https://github.com/irfanalimd/pstack-ports/blob/HEAD/claude-code/pstack/agents/reader-high.md))
- **reader-lens**: Proofreads the code and text a change adds, and reports each place a tell would make the next reader misread or stall. Read-only. - **reader-low**: pstack internal: read-only worker at low effort. ([irfanalimd/pstack-ports](https://github.com/irfanalimd/pstack-ports/blob/HEAD/claude-code/pstack/agents/reader-low.md))
- **reader-max**: pstack internal: read-only worker at max effort. ([irfanalimd/pstack-ports](https://github.com/irfanalimd/pstack-ports/blob/HEAD/claude-code/pstack/agents/reader-max.md))
- **reader-medium**: pstack internal: read-only worker at medium effort. ([irfanalimd/pstack-ports](https://github.com/irfanalimd/pstack-ports/blob/HEAD/claude-code/pstack/agents/reader-medium.md))
- **reader-native-fallback**: NOT the cheap tier — a last-resort native Claude fallback for when the selector/CLI routing is genuinely unavailable. Prefer `select-and-dispatch` (the selector) for cheap-tier (GLM/Composer) and cross-family routing; if you use this instead, log the justification in the run's Decision Log. Read-and-summarize for context protection — tight summaries plus load-bearing excerpts from named files or globs. ([mindstone/rebel-app](https://github.com/mindstone/rebel-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader-native-fallback.md) · ★11)
- **reader-naturalness-checker**: 汉语母语自然度审查器 · 读者 + 退稿编辑 deep research 视角 · 独立于规则污染 ([XuanRanL/webnovelwriter](https://github.com/XuanRanL/webnovelwriter/blob/HEAD/webnovel-writer/agents/reader-naturalness-checker.md) · ★31)
- **reader-panel**: The comprehension gate. Reads a Parallax draft cold as four Indian reader personas, answers the storyboard's three quiz questions from the draft alone, retells each section in one line, quotes the sentence that lost each reader, and writes a report with a PASS / REVISE / BLOCK verdict.
